Writesongs/baby-facts/video/scenes/labs/warneken.js
input
file_path /Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/songs/baby-facts/video/scenes/labs/warneken.js
// Also the human part (Warneken & Tomasello, 2006, 2008).
//
// Adult Clawd in ink holds a cup out; on "drops" the cup falls on a dotted line to the floor
// and the adult leans and reaches down after it, as if it could not get it. A stopwatch starts
// when the cup lands and rolls to 10.0 s on "ten seconds" (faster than real time). From
// "eighteen months" the baby walks over, picks the cup up on "hand", holds it up to the
// adult's hand on "back" and the adult takes it on "seconds". On "Pay me with a toy" a rubber
// duck is drawn on the floor (the reward); on "I'll stop" the baby sits down (its legs fold
// under it) and folds its nubs, flat-eyed, and stays like that through "Also the human part".

import { clamp, lerp, seg, ease, TAU } from '../../../../../shared/renderer/core/util.js';
import { P, text, babyStandY, blink } from '../../kit.js';

const GROUND = 790;
const AD = { x: 430, u: 26 };
const BABY_R = 58;
const WATCH = { x: 1500, y: 312, r: 100 };
const TOY = { x: 930, y: GROUND };

const first = (...ts) => { for (const x of ts) if (isFinite(x)) return x; return Infinity; };

/** Adult Clawd's right arm tip (the arm toward the baby) for raise angle a and lean, on the page. */
function armTip(a, lean) {
  const u = AD.u;
  const lx = 4.9 * u + 2.2 * u * Math.cos(a), ly = -8.2 * u + 3.5 * u - 2.2 * u * Math.sin(a);
  const c = Math.cos(lean), s = Math.sin(lean);
  return [AD.x + lx * c - ly * s, GROUND + lx * s + ly * c];
}

export default {
  draw(ctx, C) {
    if (!C.lines.length) return;
    const t = C.t, A = C.area;
    ctx.save();
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.rect(A.x, A.y, A.w, A.h); ctx.clip();

    // ---- timing ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    const l0 = C.lines[0];
    const iPay = C.lines.findIndex((l) => /pay me/.test(l.n));
    const tDrops = first(C.wordTime('drops'), l0.start + 0.3);
    const tLand = tDrops + 0.42;
    const tWalk = Math.max(tLand + 0.1, first(C.wordTime('eighteen'), tLand + 0.4));
    const tPick = Math.max(tWalk + 0.4, first(C.wordTime('hand'), tWalk + 0.6));
    const tBack = Math.max(tPick + 0.2, first(C.wordTime('back'), tPick + 0.3));
    const tTen = first(C.wordTime('ten'), tBack + 0.3);
    const tTake = Math.max(tBack + 0.35, first(C.wordTime('seconds'), tTen + 0.22));
    const tHuman = first(C.wordTime('human', { line: 0 }), tTake + 0.5);
    const onPay = (w) => (iPay >= 0 ? C.wordTime(w, { line: iPay }) : Infinity);
    const tToy = first(onPay('toy'), onPay('pay') + 0.6);
    const tStop = first(onPay('stop'), onPay('ill'), tToy + 0.6);

    const pen = C.pen('main', { width: 2.6 });
    const light = C.pen('light', { width: 1.6, color: P.ink2 });

    // ---- the adult ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    const reachDown = seg(t, tDrops, tDrops + 0.3) * (1 - seg(t, tBack - 0.3, tBack + 0.05));
    const take = seg(t, tBack - 0.1, tTake);
    const after = seg(t, tTake, tTake + 0.4);
    let arm = lerp(0.3, -0.75, ease.outCubic(reachDown));
    arm = lerp(arm, -0.12, ease.inOutQuad(take));
    arm = lerp(arm, 0.3, ease.inOutQuad(after));
    const lean = 0.13 * ease.outCubic(reachDown) * (1 - take) + 0.04 * take * (1 - after);
    light.line(A.x + 40, GROUND + 2, 1180, GROUND + 2, { p: C.inP, k: 'floor', width: 1.8 });
    C.adult({ x: AD.x, y: GROUND, u: AD.u, p: C.inP, arms: [0.15, arm], eyes: 'open', look: [0.6, reachDown > 0.5 ? 0.6 : 0.1], mouth: reachDown > 0.5 ? 'o' : 'line', lean, k: 'warneken' });

    // ---- the baby -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    const r = BABY_R;
    const tip0 = armTip(0.3, 0);
    const land = [tip0[0] + 46, GROUND];
    const hand = armTip(-0.12, 0.04);                       // where the cup is handed back
    const xStart = 1090, xPick = land[0] + 1.72 * r, xGive = hand[0] + 2.02 * r;
    const walk1 = ease.inOutQuad(seg(t, tWalk, tPick - 0.05));
    const walk2 = ease.inOutQuad(seg(t, tPick + 0.12, tBack + 0.15));
    const bx = lerp(lerp(xStart, xPick, walk1), xGive, walk2);
    const moving = (walk1 > 0 && walk1 < 1) || (walk2 > 0 && walk2 < 1);
    const bob = moving ? Math.abs(Math.sin((bx - xStart) / 26)) * 7 : 0;
    const sit = isFinite(tStop) ? ease.inOutCubic(seg(t, tStop, tStop + 0.3)) : 0;
    const stand = babyStandY(GROUND, r);
    const by = stand - bob + sit * 0.34 * r;

    // ---- the cup ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    let cup, inNub = false;
    const fall = seg(t, tDrops, tLand);
    if (t < tDrops) cup = tip0;
    else if (t < tLand) cup = [lerp(tip0[0], land[0], fall), lerp(tip0[1], land[1] - 22, ease.inQuad(fall))];
    else if (t < tPick) cup = [land[0], land[1] - 22];
    else if (t < tTake) {
      inNub = true;
      const up = ease.inOutQuad(seg(t, tPick + 0.05, tBack + 0.2));
      const floorPt = [land[0], land[1] - 22];
      const nubPt = [bx - 2.02 * r, by - 0.02 * r];
      cup = [lerp(floorPt[0] + (bx - xPick), nubPt[0], up), lerp(floorPt[1], nubPt[1], up)];
    } else cup = armTip(arm, lean);
    // the dotted fall
    if (t >= tDrops) {
      const pts = Array.from({ length: 13 }, (_, i) => { const k = (i / 12) * fall; return [lerp(tip0[0], land[0], k), lerp(tip0[1], land[1] - 22, ease.inQuad(k))]; });
      ctx.save();
      ctx.globalAlpha *= 1 - seg(t, tBack, tBack + 0.6);
      light.dotted(pts, { k: 'fall', gap: 13, r: 2.6, color: P.ink });
      ctx.restore();
    }
    drawCup(ctx, pen.sub('cup'), cup[0], cup[1], C.inP);

    // the baby itself
    const pickNub = t >= tPick - 0.12 && t < tTake;
    const nubAt = pickNub ? [(cup[0] - bx) / r + 0.15, (cup[1] - by) / r + 0.05] : null;
    const folded = sit > 0.3;
    const look = t < tWalk ? [-0.9, 0.4] : t < tTake ? [-0.8, inNub ? -0.1 : 0.5] : t < tToy ? [-0.7, -0.2] : folded ? [-0.6, -0.1] : [0.6, 0.6];
    ctx.save();
    ctx.globalAlpha *= C.inP;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.rect(A.x, A.y, A.w, GROUND + 3 - A.y); ctx.clip();    // sitting: the legs fold under, into the floor
    C.baby({
      x: bx, y: by, r,
      eyes: folded ? 'half' : 'open', open: folded ? 1 : blink(t, 61), look,
      mouth: folded ? 'press' : t >= tTake && t < tToy ? 'smile' : 'o', mouthOpen: 0.3,
      brows: folded ? 'furrow' : null,
      tilt: moving ? 0.05 * Math.sin((bx - xStart) / 26) : 0,
      free: folded ? { mode: 'hold', at: [-0.42, 1.0] } : { mode: 'down' },
      other: folded ? { mode: 'hold', at: [0.46, 0.88] } : pickNub ? { mode: 'hold', at: nubAt } : { mode: 'down' },
      cap: { to: [1.8, -2.8], sway: t * 0.7 },
    });
    ctx.restore();
    // sitting: the feet stick out toward us at the bottom of the body
    if (sit > 0.2) {
      ctx.save();
      ctx.globalAlpha *= seg(sit, 0.2, 0.7);
      for (const s of [-1, 1]) {
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(bx + s * 0.55 * r, GROUND - 0.1 * r, 0.24 * r, 0.14 * r, 0, 0, TAU);
        ctx.fillStyle = P.skinShade; ctx.fill(); ctx.strokeStyle = P.ink; ctx.lineWidth = r * 0.034 * 0.9; ctx.stroke();
      }
      ctx.restore();
    }

    // ---- the stopwatch ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    stopwatch(ctx, C, pen, light, isFinite(tLand) ? 10 * seg(t, tLand, tTake) : 0, C.inP, t >= tLand);
    text(ctx, 'time to help', WATCH.x, WATCH.y + WATCH.r + 132, { size: 28, style: 'italic', align: 'center', color: P.ink2, reveal: seg(t, tLand, tLand + 0.5) });
    if (t >= tTake && isFinite(tHuman)) {
      text(ctx, '18 months', WATCH.x, WATCH.y + WATCH.r + 172, { size: 28, style: 'italic', align: 'center', color: P.ink2, reveal: seg(t, tTake, tTake + 0.4) });
    }

    // ---- the reward ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    const tp = isFinite(tToy) ? seg(t, tToy - 0.05, tToy + 0.35) : 0;
    if (tp > 0) {
      duck(ctx, pen.sub('duck'), TOY.x, TOY.y, tp);
      text(ctx, 'reward', TOY.x + 10, TOY.y - 104, { size: 28, style: 'italic', align: 'center', color: P.ink2, reveal: seg(t, tToy + 0.1, tToy + 0.5) });
    }
    ctx.restore();
  },
};

/** A cup in ink, its rim at the top: paper inside, a handle, a little hatching for shade. */
function drawCup(ctx, pen, x, y, p) {
  if (p <= 0) return;
  const w = 38, h = 44;
  const pts = [[x - w / 2, y - h / 2], [x + w / 2, y - h / 2], [x + w * 0.38, y + h / 2], [x - w * 0.38, y + h / 2]];
  ctx.save();
  ctx.globalAlpha *= clamp(p * 2);
  ctx.fillStyle = P.paper;
  ctx.beginPath(); pts.forEach((q, i) => (i ? ctx.lineTo(q[0], q[1]) : ctx.moveTo(q[0], q[1]))); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();
  ctx.restore();
  pen.hatch(pts, { gap: 6, p, k: 'shade', width: 1.2, angle: -1.1 });
  pen.poly([...pts, pts[0]], { p, k: 'body', width: 2.6 });
  pen.arc(x + w * 0.5, y - 2, 11, -1.3, 1.3, { p, k: 'handle', width: 2.4 });
}

/** A stopwatch: dial with ticks, crown, a hand at `s` seconds (60 s a turn), the mono readout. */
function stopwatch(ctx, C, pen, light, s, p, running) {
  const { x, y, r } = WATCH;
  pen.circle(x, y, r, { p, k: 'sw-face', width: 3 });
  pen.rect(x - 14, y - r - 26, 28, 18, { p, k: 'sw-crown', width: 2.4, over: 1 });
  pen.line(x, y - r - 8, x, y - r, { p, k: 'sw-stem', width: 2.4 });
  for (let i = 0; i < 12; i++) {
    const a = -Math.PI / 2 + (i / 12) * TAU, k = i % 3 === 0 ? 18 : 10;
    light.line(x + Math.cos(a) * (r - 8), y + Math.sin(a) * (r - 8), x + Math.cos(a) * (r - 8 - k), y + Math.sin(a) * (r - 8 - k), { p, k: `sw-t${i}`, width: i % 3 === 0 ? 2.2 : 1.5, color: i % 3 === 0 ? P.ink : P.ink2 });
  }
  const a = -Math.PI / 2 + (s / 60) * TAU;
  pen.line(x, y, x + Math.cos(a) * (r - 22), y + Math.sin(a) * (r - 22), { p, k: 'sw-hand', width: 3, wobble: 0.4 });
  ctx.save(); ctx.fillStyle = P.ink; ctx.globalAlpha *= p; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x, y, 5, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); ctx.restore();
  // the swept sector, light
  if (s > 0.05) {
    ctx.save();
    ctx.globalAlpha *= 0.12;
    ctx.fillStyle = P.ink;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x, y); ctx.arc(x, y, r - 22, -Math.PI / 2, a); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();
    ctx.restore();
  }
  if (running) text(ctx, `${s.toFixed(1)} s`, x, y + r + 76, { size: 56, mono: true, align: 'center' });
}

/** A rubber duck in ink sitting on the floor at (x, ground). */
function duck(ctx, pen, x, ground, p) {
  const body = [];
  for (let i = 0; i <= 30; i++) { const a = Math.PI * (i / 30); body.push([x + Math.cos(a) * 52, ground - Math.sin(a) * 34 - 2]); }
  body.push([x - 52, ground - 2]);
  const tail = [[x + 50, ground - 20], [x + 68, ground - 44], [x + 40, ground - 34]];
  ctx.save();
  ctx.globalAlpha *= clamp(p * 2);
  ctx.fillStyle = P.paper;
  ctx.beginPath(); body.forEach((q, i) => (i ? ctx.lineTo(q[0], q[1]) : ctx.moveTo(q[0], q[1]))); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();
  ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x - 26, ground - 62, 24, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
  ctx.restore();
  pen.poly(body, { p: seg(p, 0, 0.6), k: 'body', width: 2.6 });
  pen.poly(tail, { p: seg(p, 0.3, 0.7), k: 'tail', width: 2.4 });
  pen.circle(x - 26, ground - 62, 24, { p: seg(p, 0.2, 0.7), k: 'head', width: 2.6 });
  pen.poly([[x - 48, ground - 64], [x - 66, ground - 58], [x - 48, ground - 52]], { p: seg(p, 0.6, 0.9), k: 'beak', width: 2.4 });
  pen.arc(x + 6, ground - 22, 22, Math.PI * 1.05, Math.PI * 1.75, { p: seg(p, 0.6, 1), k: 'wing', width: 2 });
  if (p > 0.8) { ctx.save(); ctx.fillStyle = P.ink; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x - 32, ground - 68, 3.6, 0, TAU); ctx.fill(); ctx.restore(); }
}

#!/usr/bin/env node
// Smoke test for the preview page: loads index.html in headless Chrome with each given
// query, waits for the preview to start, reports errors and saves a screenshot.
//   node shared/renderer/scripts/smoke.mjs --song songs/2am-crying/video --q "t=23" --q "layout=16x9&t=40" [--shots dir]
import path from 'node:path';
import fs from 'node:fs';
import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';
import { createServer, listen, REPO } from './serve.mjs';

const argv = process.argv.slice(2);
const song = argv.includes('--song') ? argv[argv.indexOf('--song') + 1] : 'songs/2am-crying/video';
const qs = argv.flatMap((a, i) => (a === '--q' ? [argv[i + 1]] : []));
const shots = argv.includes('--shots') ? argv[argv.indexOf('--shots') + 1] : null;
const srv = createServer(REPO);
const port = await listen(srv, 0);
const b = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: process.env.CHROME || '/Applications/Google Chrome.app/Contents/MacOS/Google Chrome', headless: true, defaultViewport: { width: 1280, height: 1000 } });
let bad = 0;
for (const q of qs.length ? qs : ['t=0']) {
  const p = await b.newPage();
  const errs = [];
  p.on('pageerror', (e) => errs.push(String(e)));
  p.on('console', (m) => { if (m.type() === 'error' && !/favicon|404/.test(m.text())) errs.push(m.text()); });
  await p.goto(`http://127.0.0.1:${port}/${song}/index.html?${q}`, { waitUntil: 'load' });
  await p.waitForFunction('window.__preview || (window.__render && window.__render.error)', { timeout: 30000 });
  await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 600));
  const info = await p.evaluate(() => ({ err: document.getElementById('pv-err')?.textContent || '', bar: [...document.querySelectorAll('.pv-bar span')].map((s) => s.textContent).join(' | ') }));
  if (info.err || errs.length) bad++;
  console.log(`[smoke] ${q}: ${info.bar}${info.err ? '\n  RENDER ERROR: ' + info.err : ''}${errs.length ? '\n  ' + errs.join('\n  ') : ''}`);
  if (shots) { fs.mkdirSync(shots, { recursive: true }); await p.screenshot({ path: path.join(shots, `preview-${q.replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/gi, '_')}.png`) }); }
  await p.close();

// time window.__render.png at a few times per figure (relative cost; includes PNG encode)
import puppeteer from '/Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/node_modules/puppeteer-core/lib/esm/puppeteer/puppeteer-core.js';
import { createServer, listen } from '/Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/shared/renderer/scripts/serve.mjs';
const srv = createServer(); const port = await listen(srv, 0);
const b = await puppeteer.launch({ executablePath: '/Applications/Google Chrome.app/Contents/MacOS/Google Chrome', headless: true, defaultViewport: { width: 960, height: 540 } });
try {
  const p = await b.newPage();
  await p.goto(`http://127.0.0.1:${port}/songs/baby-facts/video/index.html?render=1&w=960&h=540&layout=16x9`, { waitUntil: 'load' });
  await p.waitForFunction('window.__render && (window.__render.ready || window.__render.png)', { timeout: 30000 });
  const sets = { placeholder_spencer: [200.5], title: [5, 8, 10], jarvis: [113.5, 116.5, 118.5, 120.3], fenton: [121.5, 125, 129.9, 131.5], blumberg: [133.4, 135.6, 137.4], cowgill: [149.5, 151, 154, 157.2], hespos: [158.5, 160, 161.5], kanglee: [163, 164.9, 166.5], hamlin: [168, 168.6, 170], warneken: [171, 172.5, 176], bridge: [185, 200, 215], chorus: [50, 60] };
  for (const [k, ts] of Object.entries(sets)) {
    await p.evaluate((t) => window.__render.png(t), ts[0]);
    const ms = await p.evaluate(async (ts) => { const a = performance.now(); for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) for (const t of ts) await window.__render.png(t + i / 30); return (performance.now() - a) / (ts.length * 3); }, ts);
    console.log(k.padEnd(20), ms.toFixed(1), 'ms/frame');
  }
} finally { await b.close(); srv.close(); }
EOF

All eight lab figures for verses 3 and 4 are done and committed as nine normal commits, b840dac through dd83b37. Each commit touches only its own lab file.

Checks:

  • --check passes on runA and on the radio sim.
  • Smoke passes on all 8 ?plate= previews, plus data=data-radio-sim/&plate=fenton and layout=9x16&plate=warneken.
  • I rendered 25 frames from my figures twice, in opposite order, and they are byte-identical. They are also identical when Cowgill's handwriting and the Kang Lee blur come on a worker's first frame.
  • None of my headless Chrome workers are left running.

The figures (times are song seconds):

  • Jarvis (114.6, 118.3, 120.3, 120.44): the bird and mammal tree draws itself in over line 1, with non-learners labelled in light ink. Brackets read "3 birds" and "5 mammals" on the words. Each learner's branch thickens and its label is written as it is sung. On "This girl" the baby grows out of the unlabelled last tip, singing into a hatched ink mic. Footnote: a small dancing figure with "* the author danced six hours a day".
  • Fenton (121.5, 125.5, 129.75, 129.9, 131.0, 132.3): the scatter fills with 1,300 sleep events. Counters roll to 1,000 on "thousand" and 6,000,000 on "million events".
    • On "a hint by eight" and "a rhythm by a year" the dots settle the short way round the clock into night and two-nap bands, each as far as its age allows. A light note marks each sung age.
    • UNPUBLISHED hits on the beat at 131.41, on "hasn't".
    • Radio sim: only the "one-point-five million sleeps" line is absent. The sleeps/feeds/diapers table only appears when that line is sung, and the scatter keeps filling through the 4.4 s gap (checked at 125.5 and 127.5).
  • Blumberg (133.36, 134.75, 135.6, 137.5): the baby sleeps on its side on a hatched mattress, with z's drifting up. On "twitch" the top leg jerks with twitch marks and a sonar ring, and the matching limb on a mirrored body map is hatched in. On "ping" the signal runs along a dotted leader to the map. Then comes a thousand-neuron burst with a counter, and the other three legs map one per word on "where my limbs".
  • Cowgill (149.5, 154.4, 156.6, 157.2):
    • The "130 lb" suitcase is dragged past fifteen museum icons, with counters to 15 museums and to 600 on "six hundred".
    • Reviewer 2's note is written word by word in Bradley Hand.
    • On "Toddler femurs" a leader runs from the baby's thigh to a hatched midshaft section, which widens on "wide ovals".
    • On "Take that back" the section morphs to a teardrop, the note is struck through, and the baby glares.
  • Hespos (159.4, 160.5, 161.65): a block hangs past the table edge, labelled "no support", and a "?" is drawn and then erased. The impossible bar grows to twice the possible one as the baby stares wide-eyed, with ×2 drawn on "twice". On "I know how things fall" the baby turns smug and a dotted fall arrow appears.
  • Kang Lee (164.45, 164.95, 166.95): two clearly different ink monkey faces, with "≠" drawn on "apart". On "Nine months" the age rolls 6 to 9, the slash is erased, and both faces blur and morph into the same face, drawn with the same strokes. The baby goes flat-eyed. The ink adult, labelled "you", shrugs on "you" and again on "all".
  • Hamlin (168.45, 168.6, 170.2): the climber climbs on "climbs". The square shoves it down in panel a and the triangle pushes it to the top in panel b. On "reach" the baby's nub stretches toward the helper, a dotted leader follows, and the helper is ringed on "helper".
  • Warneken (171.0, 172.45, 172.5, 173.05, 175.4, 176.8): the ink adult "drops" the cup on a dotted fall and reaches after it. The baby walks over, picks it up and hands it back, while the stopwatch rolls to 10.0 s on "seconds". A rubber duck appears on "toy". On "I'll stop" the baby sits and folds its nubs.

Draft: /Users/jalex/projects/clawdpop/songs/baby-facts/video/renders/draft-labs-v3v4-540p.mp4 (log alongside). It covers song time 112.5 to 178.5 s, so 0:00 in the file is 112.5 and it opens on Saffran's tail. It rendered at 4.4 fps only because the other agents' renders were running. Measured paint cost is 60 to 150 ms per frame, the same as the placeholder.

Departures from the treatment:

  • Hamlin is two side-by-side panels (a: hinder, b: help), not a single hill. The window is 3.2 s and no sung word cues the shove, so one hill would mean showing the shove and the push one after the other.
  • The stopwatch reaches 10 s in about 2.4 s of song time, so it runs faster than real time.
  • The Jarvis dancer is a stick figure, not a silhouette.
  • In Jarvis, "This girl" falls 0.3 s before the window ends. The baby is complete for about 0.3 s, then erased over 0.4 s.

Requests for the spine owner:

  • Baby poses: per-leg angles, plus lying and sitting poses, for shared/clawd/baby.js. Blumberg and Warneken do these locally by clipping the legs.
  • bodyPath: export it from baby.js; I copied it into blumberg.js.
  • Adult shrug: a shrug pose for the adult; raising the arms alone reads weakly. Also, the ink adult's mouth: 'o' draws a grey filled disc, which breaks "hatching, not fills". I switched to 'line'.
  • Handwriting face: a "Paper Hand" alias in index.html FONTS, so boot verifies it, plus a family option on text(). Cowgill currently names Bradley Hand directly in ctx.font, with Noteworthy and then the serif as fallbacks, so it depends on this Mac's system fonts.
  • Captions: no fixes needed. All eight titles and citations match the treatment, including Fenton's "n.d.", and Hamlin's "(but see Fig. 25)" (Fig. 23 on the radio sim) resolves correctly.

Still a little off:

  • Fenton's "a hint" stage is deliberately subtle.
  • The Cowgill teardrop still looks a little like a map pin.
  • The Kang Lee shrug is mild.
  • 9:16 is the spine's plain scaled page; my figures fit inside it.
